The following information, published on Order No. 21515-Phila-50, supplements TM
10 November 1948. The serial numbers of the equipment covered in this supplement are:

                             Name                   Serial Numbers

Amplifier Assembly AN/FRA-1B                          1 through 8

Personnel using this equipment and having custody of this technical manual will
enter suitable notations beside each affected paragraph and figure in the technical
manual to indicate the presence of this supplementary information.

                                                                 Page 16. Par. 19e(4). Add the following after
                                                                 the last sentence:

                                                                    On Amplifier Assembly AN/FRA-1B, the
                                                                 secondary wires come off three individual in-
                                                                 sulator posts. The connection is made to
                                                                 either terminal 6 or 7 on each transformer.
                                                                 Terminal 7 is the normal operating connection
                                                                 for the equipment. Terminal 6, however, repre-
                                                                 sents a tap on the secondary winding, giving
                                                                 only 85 percent of rated plate voltage. This
                                                                 connection to terminal 6 is made only when
                                                                 it is required to operate the equipment at al-
                                                                 titudes exceeding 10,000 feet, or when local
                                                                 conditions dictate operation of the equipment
                                                                 at a lower power level. Carefully observe

of the Army Supply Catalog applicable to the equipment covered in .this manual is SIG 7&8-

of the Army Supply Catalog, see the latest issue of SIG 1, Introduction and Index.

          WARNING

         HIGH VOLTAGE

     is used in the operation of this
                    equipment.

     DEATH ON CONTACT

may result if operating personnel fail
     to observe safety precautions.

                                    SAFETY NOTICE

      Amplifier Assembly AN/FRA-1A uses extremely high voltages which are dangerous to life.
      Do not rely on the safety interlocks with which this equipment is provided; be sure voltage is off.
Do not rely on the bleeder resistors to discharge high-voltage capacitors; use the grounding hook.
      Dangerous r-f voltages are developed in the transmitter and antenna tuning unit.

                                   USE EXTREME CAUTION |

                                                     Equipment TE-87—A contains most of the neces-

                                                     up to 1%-inch hexagonal.
                                                         (4) Electrician’s pliers.
                                                        (5) Nail-puller.
                                                         (6) Hammer.
                                                        (7) Center punch.

                                                     drills. «

                                                     type.

                                                     inches long minimum.

                                                        (15) Wood rasp, large.

                                                                 Assembly AS-334/FRT is an aperiodic long-wire

                                                                 Assembly AS-334/FRT is available at this time.

                                                                  the last sentence:

                                                                     On Amplifier Assembly AN/FRA-1B, the
                                                                  secondary wires come off three individual in-
                                                                  sulator posts. The connection is made to
                                                                  either terminal 6 or 7 on each transformer.
                                                                  Terminal 7 is the normal operating connection
                                                                  for the equipment. Terminal 6, however, repre-
                                                                  sents a tap on the secondary winding, giving
                                                                  only 85 percent of rated plate voltage. This
                                                                  connection to terminal 6 is made only when
                                                                  it is required to operate the equipment at al-
                                                                  titudes exceeding 10,000 feet, or when local
                                                                  conditions dictate operation of the equipment
                                                                  at a lower power level. Carefully observe

                              APPENDIX IV
              IDENTIFICATION TABLE OF PARTS

  1. REQUISITIONING OF PARTS.
_ The fact that a part is listed in this table is not sufficient basis for requisitioning the item.

  Requisitions must cite an authorized basis, such as T/O&E, T/A, T/BA, SIG 7-8-10, SIG 10,
  list of allowances of expendable material, or another authorized supply basis. The Department
  of the Army Supply Catalog applicable te the equipment covered in.this manual is SIG 7&8-
  AN/FRA-1. For an index of available supply catalogs in the Signal portion of the Department
  of the Army Supply Catalog, see the latest issue of SIG 1, Introduction and Index.
